Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the numerical_analysis space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 170 and the convergence rate is O(310/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the numerical_analysis decomposition. By minimality, the element a^170 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 310-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
